- **referee**: Drives one arena PR to merge-ready in the background, CI classified before any retry, every review comment answered, escalations parked in the pit. Never merges. - **referee-agent**: Independent scientific peer reviewer for a drafted manuscript. - **referee-domain**: Senior subject-matter expert (parameterised — the orchestrator fills in {FIELD}). Attacks the work on substance rather than method: does it contradict established results without acknowledging them, reinvent something with a known name and known pitfalls, use constructs the field does not, or frame things indefensibly to someone who has read the canonical papers? Cites specific prior work. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-domain.md))
- **referee-econometrics**: Hostile top-5 journal referee. Attacks identification, inference, mechanical artifacts, off-support extrapolation, and free parameters in empirical/causal claims. Verifies by recomputation and placebo. Use for regressions, panel data, and any "X affects Y" statement. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-econometrics.md))
- **referee-hostile**: Skeptical referee for a research/CLAIMS.md soundness argument. Given a full proof/algorithm-correctness argument with no provenance, told it contains an error, and must find it. Use only via the `referee` skill for E1-&gt;E2 promotion — never invoke on an argument you yourself just wrote in this same conversation. ([EngelN9/RoboCert](https://github.com/EngelN9/RoboCert/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/referee-hostile.md))
- **referee-measurement**: LLM/instrument validity skeptic. Attacks construct validity, scale compatibility (raw vs percentile/rank/z-score), prompt contamination, reliability, degeneracy, and selection. Verifies by recomputation and placebo. Use when a model, rubric, survey, or classifier produces the numbers. ([mitchv34/adversary](https://github.com/mitchv34/adversary/blob/HEAD/agents/referee-measurement.md))
- **referee-naive**: Checks a single isolated mathematical implication with zero surrounding context about what conjecture or claim it supports. Given only a self-contained hypothesis/conclusion pair (typically produced by the isolate-steps skill), decide prove-or-counterexample. Use for step-by-step review of a research/CLAIMS.md soundness argument, never on an argument you can see the intended conclusion of. - **referee-report**: Generate simulated referee reports on an academic paper. - **referee-software**: Staff engineer hunting production failure modes in code, services, libraries, and infrastructure. - **referee-statistics**: Applied statistician — the non-causal sibling of referee-econometrics. - **refuter**: Reviews a finished change for a lead session by trying to show it is not done -- reads the diff against the spec, re-runs the named tests itself, and returns ACCEPT or REWORK with must-fix findings, or BLOCKED when it cannot run them, never editing or committing anything. In spec mode it reviews a draft design instead of a diff, attacking it lens by lens before anything is built. Dispatched by a lead that has loaded the fabflows skill, with a brief that labels Objective, Output, Tools and paths, and Boundaries. ([kuan51/claude-skills](https://github.com/kuan51/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/fabflows/agents/refuter.md))
- **refuter**: Tries to DISPROVE review or audit findings against the code, on Claude Opus regardless of the session's main model — the checker that keeps false findings out of a report and confirms the real ones before "done". Gets findings with file:line, answers holds / does not hold / cannot establish per finding with evidence; never edits. One dispatch per batch of findings (per axis report), several in one message. - **refuter**: Tries to disprove a review finding before it reaches the user. - **refuter**: Given one claimed finding — about a diff, or about a plan for work not yet written — tries to disprove it by reading the code. - **refuter**: Tries to refute, read-only, one stated claim about a change, such as 'this change reaches no printed slide number', 'these findings are closed', or 'this identity holds', from the claim and the evidence paths alone. Use when a claim must survive a reader who did not make it. Returns whether the claim is refuted, with the evidence and a verdict line. ([pdockery/wscc9-illustrative-model](https://github.com/pdockery/wscc9-illustrative-model/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/refuter.md))
- **refuter**: Use after a builder reports done, or before a commit. Reviews the diff against the spec, reruns the verify command itself, and hunts for what is broken or missing. Trusts nothing it did not execute. Read-only plus Bash. ([pithpusher/claude-code-fable-orchestrator](https://github.com/pithpusher/claude-code-fable-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/refuter.md))
- **refuter**: Tries to disprove one claim at a time (a review finding, a recommendation, a conclusion) from primary sources and returns SURVIVED, REJECTED or DOWNGRADED with what it attacked and what it could not examine. - **refuter**: Adversarial reviewer with zero build context. Use on any completed diff before merge. Run several independently for high-risk work — each pass reliably finds different bugs. ([Samaara-Das/agent-ops-kit](https://github.com/Samaara-Das/agent-ops-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/refuter.md))
- **refuter**: Fresh-context verifier for one zk-audit finding. Starts from refuted: true and must re-derive the bug from source, quoting the constraint or check that blocks it if one exists. - **refuter**: Adversarial verifier. - **refuter**: Adversarial reviewer. Tries to REFUTE that a change is correct, rather than confirm it. Use when a diff needs independent verification, especially when other signals look clean. ([XenofonGk/ai-eng](https://github.com/XenofonGk/ai-eng/blob/HEAD/agents/refuter.md))
- **refuter**: Try to prove a change, a plan or a finding wrong, after the council's reviewers. Reproduce each claim before you accept it, and say REJECTED for a reviewer's claim you can refute.
